→ 多模态内容理解
■ 剧本反求——腾讯 PCG 2025 管理突破奖
◆ 搭建“视觉唯堆-文本重构”多模态链路 (OCR/人脸/声纹)，实现视频内容结构化理解，结合 MLLM (Gemini/Qwen2.5-VL)自动反求视频剧本。链路全量上线，覆盖少儿 26 部/动漫 30 部/短剧 576 部，累计处理上万集，人工评测准确率超过 85%
■ 多模态内容审核

→ 音频理解与生成
■ 开发音色一致性/音质检测/声纹识别服务，全量上线 123 平台，准召提升至 75%，累计调用万次
■ 设计说话人识别管线支持剧本反求链路，融合 1.5w 影视+7w 开源数据训练 ERes2Net，准召提升 3%达到 83%并上线。基于唇形同步算法，构建说话人参考音源自动提取链路，检测准确率 >95%，支持 576 部短剧全量反求
■ 研发 zero-shot 音色克隆&多语种（泰语）TTS 算法，用于影视后期唇形修改于短剧配音，落地 5+部 S 级剧集/AIGC 短剧，并完成少儿专属音色（小企鹅）的音频能力建设（ASR+TTS）
科研经历
● 面向 Diffusion 模型的快速迁移与模型轻量化技术研究 （负责人：黄高） 快手&清华 科研合作项目
→ 研究 Stable Diffusion 各模块性质，系统性地探索了其在生成中的作用及资源消耗。针对 SD 提出通用的模型压缩(Tuning)的轻量化方法以及特征继承(Tuning-Free)的加速策略，在 T2I、I2I 等生成任务上实现采样速度提升 20%-40%，显著优化模型效率。沉淀成果并发表 SCI 1 区 Top 论文 1 篇

End to End production level machine learning system prototyping, development and maintenance: As the 3rd data scientist in the company, I worked together with director of data science and senior data scientist to build the company’s whole scalable production machine learning model and data pipeline from scratch. Including raw data ETL from AWS redshift, missing data imputation, data cleansing and transformation, new feature creation, model training and evaluation, model deploy to AWS S3. I am also in charge of the maintenance of all the model development codebase on GitHub. 2. UPLIFT campaign modeling project: UPLIFT is a completely new data driven product of true[X], which aims to reduce the ads load on the publisher while increase the attentions rate from the viewer. We used the XGBoost and lightGBM model to make the prediction on user’s sentiment and improved the ads targeting accuracy. We increased main brand survey KPI by 2-5% while reducing the ads load by 20%. This work has been filed US patent and extended to all the ads sold on FOX network platform like FOX, FX, NationalGeo, Hulu etc. 3. Work with the research analytics team to evaluate our model’s performance for all on-going campaigns: I leveraged the data collaboration platform-dataiku’s scheduling capability and integrated with google spreadsheet API to build an automatically model performance tracking dashboard host on google spreadsheet and AWS S3, which also served as an early warning system to address the possible problems such as survey distribution screws, ads and survey delivery imbalance etc. This work was highly praised by the C-level leaders in the company. 4. Help mentor junior data scientists and summer interns: Currently, our team have doubled the size, I participated in interviewing all the candidates and onboarding the newly hired junior data scientist to the team.
